2025-02-25T22:13:43.9208585Z On Linux, Open MPI is built with UCX support but it is disabled by default.
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9209637Z To enable it, first install UCX (conda install -c conda-forge ucx).
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9210145Z Afterwards, set the environment variables
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9210540Z OMPI_MCA_pml=ucx OMPI_MCA_osc=ucx
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9210908Z before launching your MPI processes.
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9211414Z Equivalently, you can set the MCA parameters in the command line:
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9211925Z mpiexec --mca pml ucx --mca osc ucx ...
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9212184Z 
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9212189Z 
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9212454Z On Linux, Open MPI is built with CUDA awareness but it is disabled by default.
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9213014Z To enable it, please set the environment variable
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9213439Z OMPI_MCA_opal_cuda_support=true
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9213811Z before launching your MPI processes.
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9214282Z Equivalently, you can set the MCA parameter in the command line:
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9214776Z mpiexec --mca opal_cuda_support 1 ...
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9215291Z Note that you might also need to set UCX_MEMTYPE_CACHE=n for CUDA awareness via
2025-02-25T22:13:43.9215899Z UCX. Please consult UCX documentation for further details.
